# Kernel regression rig

Generated by `cargo xtask kernel-cases`. Every geometry case the old kernel (truck) got wrong, as an issue, a PR or a crash report recorded it, is a use case in [`tests/kernel-cases/`](../tests/kernel-cases/) with its own right answer: numbers from analysis or a hand calculation (**analytic**) where the geometry has them, and otherwise the invariants (**invariants**): the operation succeeds, or refuses cleanly where that is the right answer; each body is one closed shell that passes the record's structure and geometry checks and SMLib's own check; the expected face, edge and vertex counts. Never a kernel's output, and never a commercial kernel's.

The cases run on SMLib, SolidCraft's kernel. A failing case stays failing until the kernel is fixed; there is no workaround on SolidCraft's side. A failed SMLib call leaves its inputs for replay in the run's output folder (`target/kernel-cases/dumps/<area>-<case>/`). See [`tests/kernel-cases/README.md`](../tests/kernel-cases/README.md) for the format.

## Coverage

| Area | Cases | Results |
|---|---|---|
| booleans | 89 | 14 fail, 1 hang, 73 pass, 1 unsupported |
| crashes | 10 | 3 fail, 1 hang, 6 pass |
| fillets | 50 | 6 fail, 41 pass, 3 unsupported |
| import | 23 | 1 fail, 22 pass |
| sheet-metal | 57 | 4 fail, 1 hang, 52 pass |
| shell | 18 | 5 fail, 13 pass |
| sweeps | 83 | 1 crash, 7 fail, 1 hang, 74 pass |
| tessellation | 14 | 1 fail, 13 pass |
| threads | 28 | 3 fail, 2 hang, 23 pass |

372 cases from 473 sources; answers: 324 analytic, 48 invariants.
